Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in poincare theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 367 and the invariant I(A,B) = 979 mod 1.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_367(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 367. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^979(S, F) is trivial. QED.
